(rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic_assert(sizeof(Body) == 6 * sizeof(double), \"Body must be packed\");\nstatic_assert(sizeof(double3) == 3 * sizeof(double), \"Position must MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n}\n\nconstexpr int TILE = 256;\n\n// Each rank owns target bodies, but every target needs all source positions.\n// All threads participate in tile loads, including the final partial block.\n__global__ void computeForces(const double3* positions, double3* velocities,\n                              int n, int offset, int count) {\n    __shared__ double3 velocities[i].z * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    \n    // Kinetic energy (assuming #pragma omp parallel for reduction(+:energy) schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        \n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), \"launching integration kernel\");\n        }\n        // Only positions cross the network; velocities remain on their owning GPU.\n        if (step + 1 < numSteps &&
